29 /**
30 * @brief Tensor addition with cuTENSOR/TBLIS/nda dispatch.
31 *
32 * @details This function performs a general tensor addition of the form
33 * \f[
34 * B_{\text{idx}_B} \leftarrow \alpha \, A_{\text{idx}_A} + \beta \, B_{\text{idx}_B} \;,
35 * \f]
36 * where \f$ \alpha \f$ and \f$ \beta \f$ are scalars, and \f$ A \f$ and \f$ B \f$ are tensors of arbitrary (possibly
37 * different) rank. The index strings specify the einsum-style mapping between dimensions of \f$ A \f$ and \f$ B \f$;
38 * indices present in one tensor but absent from the other drive broadcast/reduction on the backend.
39 *
40 * <details>
41 * <summary>**Dispatch order and details**:</summary>
42 * - If the input arrays satisfy nda::mem::have_device_compatible_addr_space, cuTENSOR's elementwise binary operation
43 * is used.
44 * - If TBLIS is available, `tblis_tensor_add` is used.
45 * - Otherwise, fallback to nda expression assignment, i.e. `b = alpha * a + beta * b`.
46 *
47 * The nda host fallback requires identical ranks and identical index strings. For the other backend paths, all inputs
48 * are forwarded as-is.
49 * </details>
50 *
51 * @note \f$ A \f$ is allowed to be a lazy conjugate expression (see nda::blas_lapack::is_conj_array_expr).
52 *
53 * @tparam A nda::blas_lapack::BlasArrayOrConj type.
54 * @tparam B nda::blas_lapack::BlasArrayFor<A> type.
55 * @param alpha Input scalar \f$ \alpha \f$.
56 * @param a Input tensor \f$ A \f$.
57 * @param idx_a Index string \f$ \text{idx}_A \f$ for tensor \f$ A \f$.
58 * @param beta Input scalar \f$ \beta \f$.
59 * @param b Input/Output tensor \f$ B \f$.
60 * @param idx_b Index string \f$ \text{idx}_B \f$ for tensor \f$ B \f$.
61 */
62 template <BlasArrayOrConj A, BlasArrayFor<A> B>
63 105 void add(get_value_t<A> alpha, A const &a, std::string_view idx_a, get_value_t<A> beta, B &&b, std::string_view idx_b) { // NOLINT
64 // compile-time checks
65 105 constexpr bool run_on_device = mem::have_device_compatible_addr_space<A, B>;
66 static_assert(!run_on_device || have_cutensor, "nda::tensor::add: cuTENSOR support is required");
67 static_assert(run_on_device || have_tblis || get_rank<A> == get_rank<B>, "nda::tensor::add: host fallback requires identical ranks");
68
69 // dispatch to backends
70 if constexpr (run_on_device) {
71 device::elementwise_binary(alpha, a, idx_a, beta, b, idx_b, b);
72 } else if constexpr (have_tblis) {
73 tblis::add(alpha, a, idx_a, beta, b, idx_b);
74 } else {
75

80 /**
81 * @brief Out-of-place tensor addition with cuTENSOR/TBLIS/nda dispatch.
82 *
83 * @details This function performs a general out-of-place tensor addition of the form
84 * \f[
85 * C_{\text{idx}_C} \leftarrow \alpha \, A_{\text{idx}_A} + \beta \, B_{\text{idx}_B} \;,
86 * \f]
87 * where \f$ \alpha \f$ and \f$ \beta \f$ are scalars, and \f$ A \f$, \f$ B \f$ and \f$ C \f$ are tensors of arbitrary
88 * (possibly different) rank. The result is written into the separate output tensor \f$ C \f$ (any prior contents of
89 * \f$ C \f$ are overwritten). The index strings specify the einsum-style mapping between operand dimensions; indices
90 * present in some operands but absent from others drive broadcast/reduction on the backend.
91 *
92 * <details>
93 * <summary>**Dispatch order and details**:</summary>
94 * - If the input arrays satisfy nda::mem::have_device_compatible_addr_space, cuTENSOR's elementwise binary operation
95 * is used.
96 * - If TBLIS is available, `tblis_tensor_add` is used twice.
97 * - Otherwise, fallback to nda expression assignment, i.e. `c = alpha * a + beta * b`.
98 *
99 * The nda host fallback requires identical ranks and identical index strings. For the other backend paths, all inputs
100 * are forwarded as-is.
101 * </details>
102 *
103 * @note \f$ A \f$ and \f$ B \f$ are allowed to be lazy conjugate expressions (see
104 * nda::blas_lapack::is_conj_array_expr).
105 *
106 * @tparam A nda::blas_lapack::BlasArrayOrConj type.
107 * @tparam B nda::blas_lapack::BlasArrayOrConjFor<A> type.
108 * @tparam C nda::blas_lapack::BlasArrayFor<A> type.
109 * @param alpha Input scalar \f$ \alpha \f$.
110 * @param a Input tensor \f$ A \f$.
111 * @param idx_a Index string \f$ \text{idx}_A \f$ for tensor \f$ A \f$.
112 * @param beta Input scalar \f$ \beta \f$.
113 * @param b Input tensor \f$ B \f$.
114 * @param idx_b Index string \f$ \text{idx}_B \f$ for tensor \f$ B \f$.
115 * @param c Output tensor \f$ C \f$.
116 * @param idx_c Index string \f$ \text{idx}_C \f$ for tensor \f$ C \f$.
117 */
118 template <BlasArrayOrConj A, BlasArrayOrConjFor<A> B, BlasArrayFor<A> C>
119 32 void add(get_value_t<A> alpha, A const &a, std::string_view idx_a, get_value_t<A> beta, B const &b, std::string_view idx_b, C &&c, // NOLINT
120 std::string_view idx_c) {
121 // compile-time checks
122 32 constexpr bool run_on_device = mem::have_device_compatible_addr_space<A, B, C>;
123 static_assert(!run_on_device || have_cutensor, "nda::tensor::add: cuTENSOR support is required");
124 static_assert(run_on_device || have_tblis || (get_rank<A> == get_rank<B> && get_rank<A> == get_rank<C>),
125 "nda::tensor::add: host fallback requires identical ranks");
126
127 // dispatch to backends
128 if constexpr (run_on_device) {
129 device::elementwise_binary(alpha, a, idx_a, beta, b, idx_b, c, binary_op::SUM);
130 } else if constexpr (have_tblis) {
131 tblis::add(beta, b, idx_b, get_value_t<A>{0}, c, idx_c);
132 tblis::add(alpha, a, idx_a, get_value_t<A>{1}, c, idx_c);
133 } else {
